# Issuer census (phase 1)

Phase 1 of `crawler/FULL_CRAWL.md`. Every entry on the Malaysia and Singapore regulator lists was checked against the institution's own official site on 2026-11-11. Every entry on the Hong Kong Monetary Authority authorized-institution and digital-bank lists was checked against the institution's own official site on 2026-11-13, where an official website was stated. Sources are the regulator sites and the institutions' own sites only. `null` means not known or not applicable.

Results: `in_repository`, `same_group`, `new_issuer`, `no_consumer_card`, `out_of_scope_wholesale`, `not_reached` (see FULL_CRAWL.md, Phase 1, step 3). `out_of_scope_wholesale` is a Singapore scope exclusion for a MAS FID Wholesale Bank, by maintainer decision; it is not a confirmed product exclusion. An `off_domain_redirect` was recorded and not followed.

## Evidence follow-up (2026-11-11)

This pass kept the phase 1 review edits for Ryt, Paydee, sg-trust, my-hsbc, my-aeon-credit and sg-dbs. It did not add a result label. Home-market or global pages are not counted below as confirmed Singapore exclusions.

Maybank Islamic Berhad is now same_group under my-maybank. The catalogue at https://www.maybank.com/islamic/en/cards/islamic_cards_listing.page names consumer Ikhwan credit cards and carries a Maybank Islamic Berhad copyright. MBSB Bank Berhad, a special check outside the MY list totals, is no_consumer_card. Its approved destination https://www.mbsb.com/mbsbbank/ lists Visa Debit Card-i and MBSB Tap 2 Pay only. UBS AG stays no_consumer_card on the Singapore wealth page https://www.ubs.com/sg/en/wealthmanagement/what-we-offer.html, which does not list a credit or charge card.

These decisions are not confirmed. Bank of China (Malaysia) Berhad's own catalogue lists only a debit card, while the BNM entry still says Credit Card Issuer. HL Bank's Singapore credit-cards path returns the site error page, and the menu link points at hlb.com.my. Citibank N.A. remains same_group sg-citi only because the cards sit on citibank.com.sg. Those pages name Citibank Singapore and do not name Citibank N.A. or Citibank Singapore Limited. HDFC's approved destination https://www.hdfc.bank.in/ is the India personal site. SMBC's homepage is the Japan site. Neither establishes a Singapore consumer card.

23 SG no_consumer_card rows are not confirmed exclusions: Bank of America, MUFG Bank, PT Bank Negara Indonesia, Agricultural Bank of China, BBVA, Bank of Taiwan, Barclays, Chang Hwa, China CITIC, China Merchants Bank, Commonwealth Bank of Australia, E.SUN, First Abu Dhabi Bank, First Commercial Bank, Habib Bank, Land Bank of Taiwan, Mega International Commercial Bank, Bank of Yokohama, Chiba Bank, Chugoku Bank, Wells Fargo, Westpac and Woori Bank. A label is needed for a page that was read, or could not be reread, where Singapore product scope is not established. Until that label exists, those rows keep no_consumer_card in the table and are left out of the confirmed count.

Confirmed counts after that exclusion: MY list entries in_repository 23, same_group 7, new_issuer 1, no_consumer_card 8, not_reached 0, with Bank of China MY unconfirmed. MY unique institutions in_repository 18, same_group 7, new_issuer 1, no_consumer_card 8, not_reached 0. SG in_repository 15, same_group 3, new_issuer 0, no_consumer_card 43, not_reached 52. The table below still shows 66 SG no_consumer_card rows because it counts the unconfirmed 23 as well. 43 plus 23 is 66. Note: a later maintainer decision on 2026-11-11 confirmed 18 of the wholesale-bank rows in that unconfirmed list; the three Full Bank rows and the current counts are in the sections below.

Retries that stayed not_reached: Agrobank (timeout after the earlier TLS failure), Diners Club MY (www host did not resolve, apex host timed out), American Express MY (off-domain redirect to network.americanexpress.com, not followed), BNP Paribas (redirect to apac.bnpparibas, not followed), China Construction Bank (TLS), Bangkok Bank (timeout), Bank of India (HTTP 403 challenge), Indian Bank (timeout), Indian Overseas Bank (timeout), UCO Bank (TLS certificate), Optal (redirect to wexinc.com, not followed). ## Hong Kong (HK)

Regulator lists read on hkma.gov.hk, as at 31 October 2026: List of Licensed Banks (147), List of Restricted Licence Banks (17), List of Deposit-taking Companies (11), and List of Digital Banks (8). The official contact list addr.xls gives each authorized institution's official website, where stated. The eight digital banks are also on the List of Licensed Banks, so they have one row for each list. Unique authorized institutions: 175. List entries in the table: 183.

The HKMA page on the three-tier banking system (last revision 02 November 2026) states that Local Representative Offices are not allowed to engage in any banking business. The List of Local Representative Offices (40 names, as at 31 October 2026) was therefore not treated as a list of potential card issuers and is not in the table.

The interactive Register of AIs and LROs on vpr.hkma.gov.hk, and the registers index on hkma.gov.hk, were not read: `pipeline/fetcher.py` treats any URL containing the substring "register" as `login_required`. The Excel lists linked from the official three-tier and digital-banks pages were used instead.

When a homepage was a JavaScript shell, English or card URLs written in that shell HTML were first fetched in plain mode. Where that was not enough, ordinary browser mode was used (never real-Chrome). Ant Bank's official homepage still returned no readable body in ordinary browser mode.

## Thailand (TH)

Regulator lists read on bot.or.th on 2026-11-13 (Asia/Kuala_Lumpur): the list of financial institutions and non-banks supervised by the Bank of Thailand, at https://www.bot.or.th/th/fi-list.html. The list is a JavaScript component. The complete bank list (111 unique institutions across six pages at page size 20) and the licensed non-bank credit card operators (license filter 0700, 8 institutions) were read from the official same-origin listing JSON on bot.or.th, after an ordinary-browser render of the list page (cookie notice click only) showed the working request shape. The unfiltered non-bank list has 5147 entries (money changers and other licenses). Those other licenses were not treated as credit card operators and were not paginated.

The BOT supervision PDF used for representative-office scope is https://www.bot.or.th/content/dam/bot/documents/th/our-roles/financial-institutions/financial-business-under-the-bot-supervision-th.pdf. app.bot.or.th is linked from bot.or.th but is a different host and was not fetched. Official domains were added only after a BOT page stated them. countries.json already has Thailand (TH, THB, Asia/Bangkok); it was not changed.

Results in this section: 12 new_issuer, 79 no_consumer_card, 27 not_reached, 1 same_group, 0 in_repository, 0 out_of_scope_wholesale, 119 list entries in total.

## Indonesia (ID)

Regulator lists read on 2026-11-13 (Asia/Kuala_Lumpur). Sources were official regulator pages and each bank's own official site only. Aggregators were not used.

Bank list: OJK, Daftar Alamat Kantor Pusat Bank Umum Dan Syariah, Juni 2026, sheet Daftar Bank Umum, Excel file at https://www.ojk.go.id/id/kanal/perbankan/data-dan-statistik/Documents/Pages/Daftar-Alamat-Kantor-Pusat-Bank-Umum-Dan-Syariah/Daftar%20Alamat%20Kantor%20Pusat%20Bank%20Umum%20Dan%20Syariah%20-%20Juni%202026.xlsx (4 Persero, 67 Swasta, 27 BPD, 7 foreign branches; 105 banks, each with a website). Footnotes in that workbook are notes, not institutions. OJK https robots.txt was 404 (no rules).

Finance-company directory: OJK, Direktori Lembaga Pembiayaan dan PMV Juli 2026, PDF at https://www.ojk.go.id/id/kanal/iknb/data-dan-statistik/direktori/lembaga-pembiayaan/Documents/Direktori%20Lembaga%20Pembiayaan%20dan%20PMV%20Juli%202026.pdf (193 firms). The directory does not state websites, and it does not label any firm as a kartu kredit issuer. Individual finance-company sites were not opened. Names that include Credit, and that should be retried if an official website is later confirmed, are AEON Credit Services Indonesia, PT Home Credit Indonesia, and Credit Saison Indonesia Finance.

Bank Indonesia: robots.txt on https://www.bi.go.id/robots.txt was 200 (Allow: /, with Disallow for /_layouts, /Lists, Style Library and similar). The Daftar Lembaga Berizin page at https://www.bi.go.id/id/layanan/informasi-perizinan/sistem-pembayaran/default.aspx lists PJP licensees, but the category filter, pagination and Unduh control are ASP.NET POST forms. Those forms were not submitted. SPIP tables 5c and 2 are aggregate statistics, not an issuer list. The census therefore uses the OJK bank list plus official-site confirmation. The BI PJP list is a gap until a maintainer allows the public filter or a static list is published.

Each of the 105 bank websites was opened through `pipeline/fetcher.py` only (plain, then the ordinary browser when the plain fetch was a shell, timeout or similar, including `--disable-http2` where needed). Real-Chrome was not used. Off-domain redirects were recorded and not followed. A first-pass product-keyword tag was downgraded when the official text was bill payment, meta keywords, a government-transaction news item, or a foreign-group page rather than a local consumer card catalogue.

Proposed official hosts that were not added, because they are off-domain from the OJK website host and were not fetched: indonesia.icbc.com.cn (ICBC Indonesia card menu) and mandirikartukredit.com (Bank Mandiri card catalogue). applykartukredit.bankmega.com was seen on Bank Mega pages and was not added.

No `in_repository` or `same_group` result was recorded. Related entities such as BCA Syariah, Bank Digital BCA and Mega Syariah were not shown to sell consumer cards on a confirmed conventional-issuer catalogue, and their own sites were not confirmed as card issuers.

## Philippines (PH)

Regulator lists read on bsp.gov.ph on 2026-11-13. The official page is [Directories of supervised Financial Institutions](https://www.bsp.gov.ph/SitePages/financialstability/Directories.aspx), which names the [Directory of Banks and Non-Bank Financial Institutions](https://www.bsp.gov.ph/SitePages/FinancialStability/DirBanksFIList.aspx). The directory is a searchable list. The types and the institution rows were read from the official SharePoint lists that that page loads (`Financial Institution` and `Institutions`) on the same host. Plain requests to bsp.gov.ph returned HTTP 403 (Akamai Access Denied, class `bot_wall`, `robots.txt` also 403 / `robots_unreadable`). Ordinary browser mode returned the pages and the list data as Atom XML. Real-Chrome mode was not used.

The directory filter All Banks is Universal and Commercial Banks (44), Thrift Banks (41), and Rural and Cooperative Banks (366): 451 entries. Digital Banks (7) and Credit Card Company (4) are separate type filters on the same directory. Unique institutions in this census: 462. A bank appears in one type filter only.

`new_issuer` is used only where an official page of the institution states a consumer credit or charge card. `same_group` is used where the BSP directory gives the same official website as another issuer. `no_consumer_card` is used only where the official product list or site map was read and did not list a consumer credit or charge card. Other entries are `not_reached`, with the reason in the note. Comparison sites were not used. A website that is not stated in the BSP directory was not invented from an email domain or from memory.
